    case IMU_INIT_ERR_LIB:     return "library init failed";
    case IMU_INIT_ERR_HANG:    return "library init did not finish";
    case IMU_INIT_ERR_REPORTS: return "too few gyro/game RV reports";
    default:                   return "?";
    }
}

// ─── Thread-safe getter ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
extern "C" bool imu_get(imu_data_t *out) {
    if (!out || !g_mutex) return false;
    if (xSemaphoreTake(g_mutex, pdMS_TO_TICKS(5)) != pdTRUE) return false;
    fold_pending_reset();
    *out = g_data;
    xSemaphoreGive(g_mutex);
    return out->valid;
}

===== FILE: AGV_MECANUM/src/main.c =====
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"
#include "freertos/semphr.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"
#include <string.h>

#include "motor/motor.h"
#include "encoder/encoder.h"
#include "ibus/ibus.h"
#include "kinematics/kinematics.h"
#include "logger/logger.h"
#include "wifi/wifi.h"
#include "imu/imu.h"
#include "led_buzzer/led_buzzer.h"
#include "traj_receiver/traj_receiver.h"
#include "ultrasonic/ultrasonic.h"
#include "manual_control/manual_control.h"
#include "controller/vel_ctrl.h"
#include "tests/test_runner.h"

static const char *TAG = "MAIN";

// ─── Controller mode: CTRL_MODE trong controller/vel_ctrl.h ──────────────────

// ─── IBus channel (tay cầm đếm từ 1, index = channel - 1) ───────────────────
#define CH_LX   0   // channel 1 — left stick X  → vy
#define CH_LY   1   // channel 2 — left stick Y  → vx
#define CH_RY   2   // channel 3 — right stick Y (unused)
#define CH_RX   3   // channel 4 — right stick X → wz
#define CH_SWC  4   // channel 5 — nấc 3 (>1700) → MANUAL
#define CH_VRA  5   // channel 6 — speed scale
#define CH_SWA  6   // channel 7 — motor enable
#define CH_SWD  7   // channel 8 — buzzer
#define CH_SWB  8   // channel 9 — tắt/bật ultrasonic obstacle

// ─── Threshold ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
#define OBSTACLE_CM      20.0f      // tăng lên 20cm — US-015 không ổn định dưới 10cm
#define TRAJ_TIMEOUT_US  200000LL   // 200ms tính bằng µs

// ─── System mode ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
typedef enum {
    MODE_WAITING = 0,
    MODE_MANUAL,
    MODE_AUTO,
} system_mode_t;

// ─── Shared state ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
static volatile system_mode_t  g_mode            = MODE_WAITING;
static volatile bool           g_emergency_stop  = false;
static volatile bool           g_motor_enabled   = false;
static volatile int64_t        g_last_traj_us    = 0;

static volatile traj_setpoint_t g_setpoint        = {0};
static volatile float            g_enc_rpm[4]     = {0};
static volatile int32_t          g_enc_count[4]   = {0};
static volatile us_data_t        g_us_data        = {0};
static SemaphoreHandle_t         g_us_mutex;

static volatile bool g_obstacle_enabled = true;
static volatile bool g_imu_fail         = false;   // imu_init() lỗi: motor tắt, LED/còi báo (§7)

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// CORE 0 - traj_task (priority 3)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
static void traj_task(void *pv) {
    while (1) {
        traj_setpoint_t sp  = traj_receiver_get();
        traj_source_t   src = traj_receiver_source();
        /* traj_receiver_get() trả về {0} khi timeout.
         * Dùng g_last_us trong traj_receiver để quyết định fresh/stale
         * thay vì check source. */
        bool fresh = (traj_receiver_source() != TRAJ_SRC_NONE);
        if (fresh) {
            ESP_LOGI(TAG, "traj fresh src=%d vx=%.2f", src, sp.vx);  // thêm
            g_setpoint     = sp;
            g_last_traj_us = esp_timer_get_time();
        }
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// CORE 0 - ultrasonic_task (priority 1)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
static void ultrasonic_task(void *pv) {
    us_data_t buf[3];
    int idx = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) ultrasonic_read_all(&buf[i]);

    while (1) {
        ultrasonic_read_all(&buf[idx]);
        idx = (idx + 1) % 3;

        us_data_t filtered;
        for (int i = 0; i < ULTRASONIC_COUNT; i++) {
            int valid_count = 0;
            float vals[3];
            for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
                if (buf[j].valid[i]) vals[valid_count++] = buf[j].distance_cm[i];
            }

            if (valid_count == 0) {
                filtered.valid[i]       = false;
                filtered.distance_cm[i] = -1.0f;
            } else if (valid_count == 1) {
                filtered.valid[i]       = true;
                filtered.distance_cm[i] = vals[0];
            } else if (valid_count == 2) {
                filtered.valid[i]       = true;
                filtered.distance_cm[i] = (vals[0] + vals[1]) / 2.0f;
            } else {
                if (vals[0] > vals[1]) { float t = vals[0]; vals[0] = vals[1]; vals[1] = t; }
                if (vals[1] > vals[2]) { float t = vals[1]; vals[1] = vals[2]; vals[2] = t; }
                if (vals[0] > vals[1]) { float t = vals[0]; vals[0] = vals[1]; vals[1] = t; }
                filtered.valid[i]       = true;
                filtered.distance_cm[i] = vals[1];
            }
        }

        if (xSemaphoreTake(g_us_mutex, pdMS_TO_TICKS(5)) == pdTRUE) {
            g_us_data = filtered;
            xSemaphoreGive(g_us_mutex);
        }
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(20));
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// CORE 0 - led_task (priority 2)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
static void led_task(void *pv) {
    float hue = 0.0f;
    while (1) {
        system_mode_t mode = g_mode;
        bool emg = g_emergency_stop;

        if (g_imu_fail) {
            // IMU khởi tạo lỗi (Liam 27–28/10): LED xanh dương NHÁY + còi 1 dài 5 ngắn, lặp mỗi 3 s
            // [ĐỀ XUẤT: dài 600 ms, nghỉ 200 ms, ngắn 120/120 ms]. Khác AUTO-UART (xanh dương sáng liên tục).
            led_set_color(COLOR_BLUE);
            buzzer_beep(2700, 600);
            led_set_color(COLOR_OFF);
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(200));
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                led_set_color(COLOR_BLUE);
                buzzer_beep(2700, 120);
                led_set_color(COLOR_OFF);
                vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(120));
            }
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(1000));   // 600 + 200 + 5 × 240 + 1000 = 3000 ms
            continue;
        }

        if (emg) {
            led_set_color(COLOR_RED);
            buzzer_beep(2700, 100);
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(150));
            led_set_color(COLOR_OFF);
            buzzer_off();
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(150));
            continue;
        }

        switch (mode) {
        case MODE_WAITING:
            led_set_color(COLOR_WHITE);
            buzzer_beep(2700, 50);
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(500));
            led_set_color(COLOR_OFF);
            buzzer_off();
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(500));
            break;

        case MODE_MANUAL: {
            float h = hue / 60.0f;
            int   i = (int)h;
            float f = h - i;
            float q = 1.0f - f;
            uint8_t r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
            switch (i % 6) {
                case 0: r=255; g=(uint8_t)(255*f); b=0;           break;
                case 1: r=(uint8_t)(255*q); g=255; b=0;           break;
                case 2: r=0;   g=255; b=(uint8_t)(255*f);         break;
                case 3: r=0;   g=(uint8_t)(255*q); b=255;         break;
                case 4: r=(uint8_t)(255*f); g=0;   b=255;         break;
                case 5: r=255; g=0;   b=(uint8_t)(255*q);         break;
            }
            led_set_color((rgb_color_t){r, g, b});
            hue += 3.0f;
            if (hue >= 360.0f) hue = 0.0f;
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(30));
            break;
        }

        case MODE_AUTO:
            /* Màu theo source: UART=xanh dương, WiFi=tím */
            if (traj_receiver_source() == TRAJ_SRC_UART)
                led_set_color((rgb_color_t){0, 100, 255});
            else
                led_set_color((rgb_color_t){180, 0, 255});
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(100));
            break;
        }
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// CORE 0 - logger_task: định nghĩa trong logger.c
// ─────────────────────────────────────────

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// CORE 1 - encoder_task (priority 6)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
static void encoder_task(void *pv) {
    while (1) {
        for (int i = 0; i < ENC_COUNT; i++) {
            g_enc_rpm[i]   = encoder_get_rpm((enc_id_t)i);
            g_enc_count[i] = encoder_get_count((enc_id_t)i);
        }
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Log 100 Hz (motor bật) / 10 Hz (motor tắt): cột cũ + cột IMU, mất log (spec controller–IMU v2.2 §8)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
static void push_log(int64_t t_now, agv_velocity_t vel, const vel_ctrl_out_t *vc,
                     const int pwm[4], const imu_data_t *imu, bool motor_on) {
    const imu_fusion_out_t *m = &vc->imu;
    const heading_out_t    *h = &vc->head;
    log_data_t d = {
        .timestamp_us = t_now,
        .fl_count = g_enc_count[ENC_FL],
        .fr_count = g_enc_count[ENC_FR],
        .rl_count = g_enc_count[ENC_RL],
        .rr_count = g_enc_count[ENC_RR],
        .fl_rpm   = g_enc_rpm[ENC_FL],
        .fr_rpm   = g_enc_rpm[ENC_FR],
        .rl_rpm   = g_enc_rpm[ENC_RL],
        .rr_rpm   = g_enc_rpm[ENC_RR],
        .vx       = vc->actual.vx,
        .vy       = vc->actual.vy,
        .wz       = vc->actual.wz,
        .sp_vx    = vel.vx,
        .sp_vy    = vel.vy,
        .sp_wz    = vel.wz,
        .pwm_fl   = pwm[0],
        .pwm_fr   = pwm[1],
        .pwm_rl   = pwm[2],
        .pwm_rr   = pwm[3],
        .ax       = imu->accel_x,
        .ay       = imu->accel_y,
        .az       = imu->accel_z,
        .gx       = imu->gyro_x,
        .gy       = imu->gyro_y,
        .gz       = imu->gyro_z,
        .yaw      = imu->yaw,
        .pitch    = imu->pitch,
        .roll     = imu->roll,
        .tick     = vc->tick,
        .dpwm_fl  = vc->dpwm_sum[0], .dpwm_fr = vc->dpwm_sum[1],
        .dpwm_rl  = vc->dpwm_sum[2], .dpwm_rr = vc->dpwm_sum[3],
        .sat_fl   = vc->sat_ticks[0], .sat_fr = vc->sat_ticks[1],
        .sat_rl   = vc->sat_ticks[2], .sat_rr = vc->sat_ticks[3],

        .kind     = LOG_KIND_DATA,
        .motor_on = motor_on,
        .imu_init = (int8_t)imu_init_status(),
        .wz_meas  = m->wz_meas,
        .wz_cmd   = vc->wz_cmd,
        .gz_fused = m->gz,
        .yaw_rad  = m->yaw,
        .age_g_ms = m->age_g_ms,
        .age_r_ms = m->age_r_ms,
        .gyro_n   = imu->gyro_n,
        .rv_n     = imu->rv_n,
        .reset_n  = imu->reset_n,
        .g_valid  = m->g_valid,
        .r_valid  = m->r_valid,
        .wz_src   = (uint8_t)m->wz_src,
        .src_switch_count = m->src_switch_count,
        .psi_ref  = h->psi_ref,
        .e_psi    = h->e_psi,
        .head_on  = h->on,
        .psi_reset_count = h->psi_reset_count,
        .slip_flag = m->slip_flag,
        .slip_on_count = m->slip_on_count,
        .r        = m->r,
        .bias_gz  = m->bias_gz,
    };
    logger_push(&d);
}

// Hết lượt (motor bật → tắt): dòng tổng kết gửi 3 lần; tick mang log_seq của dòng dữ liệu cuối
static void push_run_end(void) {
    log_data_t d = { .kind = LOG_KIND_RUN_END, .tick = logger_seq() };
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) logger_push(&d);
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// CORE 1 - control_task (priority 5)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// IMU: control_task tự copy imu_get() mỗi tick (có mutex), thay cho imu_task cũ
// (bản sao volatile cũ có thể bị đọc lệch giữa chừng khi imu_task ưu tiên cao hơn chen vào).
static void control_task(void *pv) {
    const float dt = 0.01f;
    int64_t t_prev = esp_timer_get_time();
    bool    swa_prev = false;
    static imu_data_t s_imu;
    bool    have_imu = false;

    while (1) {
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10)); // 100Hz

        int64_t t_now   = esp_timer_get_time();
        float dt_actual = (t_now - t_prev) * 1e-6f;
        t_prev = t_now;
        if (dt_actual <= 0.0f || dt_actual > 0.1f) dt_actual = dt;

        // ── 1. Đọc IBus ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        ibus_data_t rc;
        bool has_rc = ibus_get(&rc);

        // ── 2. SWA — motor enable/disable ────────────────────────────────────
        // bool swa_on = has_rc && (rc.channel[CH_SWA] > 1500);
        // if (swa_on != swa_prev) {
        //     g_emergency_stop = false;
        //     swa_prev         = swa_on;
        // }
        // if (g_motor_enabled != swa_on) {
        //     g_motor_enabled = swa_on;
        //     motor_enable(swa_on);
        // }


        // ── 2. SWA — motor enable/disable ────────────────────────────────────
        // HIL_BYPASS_MOTOR_ENABLE comes only from env hil_bench (HIL bench has no RC
        // receiver). Vehicle firmware: SWA enables the motors, and RC loss (ibus
        // valid = false after 20 ms without a byte) disables them (spec
        // controller-IMU v2 §7b, Liam approved 27/10).
        #ifdef HIL_BYPASS_MOTOR_ENABLE
                bool swa_on = true;
        #else
                bool swa_on = has_rc && (rc.channel[CH_SWA] > 1500);
        #endif
                // IMU khởi tạo lỗi: không bao giờ bật motor (spec controller–IMU v2.2 §7)
                if (g_imu_fail) swa_on = false;
                if (swa_on != swa_prev) {
                    g_emergency_stop = false;
                    swa_prev         = swa_on;
                }
                if (g_motor_enabled != swa_on) {
                    g_motor_enabled = swa_on;
                    motor_enable(swa_on);
                }


        // ── 2b. SWB — tắt/bật obstacle detection (chỉ hoạt động ở MANUAL) ───
        if (has_rc && g_mode == MODE_MANUAL) {
            g_obstacle_enabled = (rc.channel[CH_SWB] < 1500);
        } else {
            g_obstacle_enabled = true;
        }

        // ── 3. SWD — buzzer (TODO: task riêng) ───────────────────────────────

        // ── 4. Emergency stop — ultrasonic ───────────────────────────────────
        static bool    s_obstacle      = false;
        static int64_t s_last_update   = 0;
        static int     obstacle_count  = 0;
        static int     clear_count     = 0;
        static bool    s_front_blocked = false;
        static bool    s_back_blocked  = false;
        static bool    s_left_blocked  = false;
        static bool    s_right_blocked = false;

        static us_data_t us_snap = {0};
        bool us_data_fresh = false;

        if (xSemaphoreTake(g_us_mutex, pdMS_TO_TICKS(2)) == pdTRUE) {
            us_snap = g_us_data;
            xSemaphoreGive(g_us_mutex);
            us_data_fresh = true;
        }

        if (us_data_fresh && (t_now - s_last_update > 50000LL)) {
            bool any_obstacle = false;
            bool all_clear    = true;

            if (g_obstacle_enabled) {
                s_front_blocked = us_snap.valid[US_FRONT] && us_snap.distance_cm[US_FRONT] < OBSTACLE_CM;
                s_back_blocked  = us_snap.valid[US_BACK]  && us_snap.distance_cm[US_BACK]  < OBSTACLE_CM;
                s_left_blocked  = us_snap.valid[US_LEFT]  && us_snap.distance_cm[US_LEFT]  < OBSTACLE_CM;
                s_right_blocked = us_snap.valid[US_RIGHT] && us_snap.distance_cm[US_RIGHT] < OBSTACLE_CM;
            } else {
                s_front_blocked = false;
                s_back_blocked  = false;
                s_left_blocked  = false;
                s_right_blocked = false;
            }

            any_obstacle = s_front_blocked || s_back_blocked ||
                           s_left_blocked  || s_right_blocked;
            all_clear    = !any_obstacle;

            if (any_obstacle) {
                clear_count = 0;
                obstacle_count++;
                if (obstacle_count >= 2) s_obstacle = true;
            } else {
                obstacle_count = 0;
                clear_count++;
                if (clear_count >= 3 && all_clear) s_obstacle = false;
            }
            s_last_update = t_now;
        }
        g_emergency_stop = s_obstacle;

        // ── 5. Xác định mode ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        static int s_rc_lost_counter = 0;
        bool swc_manual  = has_rc && (rc.channel[CH_SWC] > 1700);
        bool traj_fresh  = (g_last_traj_us > 0) &&
                           ((t_now - g_last_traj_us) < TRAJ_TIMEOUT_US);

        if (swc_manual) {
            g_mode = MODE_MANUAL;
            s_rc_lost_counter = 0;
        } else if (traj_fresh) {
            g_mode = MODE_AUTO;
            s_rc_lost_counter = 0;
        } else {
            if (g_mode == MODE_MANUAL && s_rc_lost_counter < 5) {
                s_rc_lost_counter++;
            } else {
                g_mode = MODE_WAITING;
            }
        }

        // ── 6. Motor off: xử lý sau bước 8–10 (vel_ctrl_step chạy mỗi tick để
        //       theo dõi hợp lệ IMU, bias khi đứng yên; spec controller–IMU v2.2 §9)

        // ── 7. Setpoint theo mode ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        agv_velocity_t vel = {0};
        switch (g_mode) {
        case MODE_MANUAL:
            if (has_rc) {
                float speed_scale = (float)(rc.channel[CH_VRA] - 1000) / 1000.0f;
                if (speed_scale < 0.0f) speed_scale = 0.0f;
                if (speed_scale > 1.0f) speed_scale = 1.0f;
                vel.vx =  ibus_channel_normalized(&rc, CH_LY) / 500.0f * speed_scale;
                vel.vy = -ibus_channel_normalized(&rc, CH_LX) / 500.0f * speed_scale;
                vel.wz = -ibus_channel_normalized(&rc, CH_RX) / 500.0f * speed_scale;
            }
            break;
        case MODE_AUTO:
            vel.vx = g_setpoint.vx;
            vel.vy = g_setpoint.vy;
            vel.wz = g_setpoint.wz;
            break;
        case MODE_WAITING:
        default:
            break;
        }

        // ── 7b. Lưu intent (trước khi interlock) ─────────────────────────────
        bool intent_fwd   = vel.vx > 0.0f;
        bool intent_bwd   = vel.vx < 0.0f;
        bool intent_left  = vel.vy > 0.0f;
        bool intent_right = vel.vy < 0.0f;

        // ── 7c. Interlock ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (s_front_blocked && vel.vx > 0.0f) vel.vx = 0.0f;
        if (s_back_blocked  && vel.vx < 0.0f) vel.vx = 0.0f;
        if (s_left_blocked  && vel.vy > 0.0f) vel.vy = 0.0f;
        if (s_right_blocked && vel.vy < 0.0f) vel.vy = 0.0f;

        // ── 8–10. FK → hợp nhất IMU + giữ hướng → controller → IK → PWM ─────
        //         (controller/vel_ctrl.c, imu_fusion.c, heading.c)
        if (imu_get(&s_imu)) have_imu = true;
        vel_ctrl_in_t vin = {
            .sp         = vel,
            .enc_rpm    = { g_enc_rpm[ENC_FL], g_enc_rpm[ENC_FR],
                            g_enc_rpm[ENC_RL], g_enc_rpm[ENC_RR] },
            .enc_count  = { g_enc_count[ENC_FL], g_enc_count[ENC_FR],
                            g_enc_count[ENC_RL], g_enc_count[ENC_RR] },
            .imu        = have_imu ? &s_imu : NULL,
            .now_us     = t_now,
            .dt         = dt_actual,
            .motor_on   = g_motor_enabled,
            .run_active = g_motor_enabled && g_mode == MODE_AUTO && traj_fresh,
#ifdef RUN_WHEELS_LIFTED
            .wheels_lifted = true,
#else
            .wheels_lifted = false,
#endif
        };
        vel_ctrl_out_t vc = vel_ctrl_step(&vin);

        static bool s_motor_prev = false;
        static int  s_idle_div   = 0;
        if (!g_motor_enabled) {
            motor_stop_all();
            traj_receiver_send_status(0, 0, 0);
            if (s_motor_prev) push_run_end();                 // hết lượt: tổng kết 3 lần (§8)
            s_motor_prev = false;
            if (++s_idle_div >= 10) {                         // dòng nhịp thưa 10 Hz khi motor tắt
                s_idle_div = 0;
                const int pwm0[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
                push_log(t_now, vel, &vc, pwm0, &s_imu, false);
            }
            continue;
        }
        s_motor_prev = true;
        s_idle_div   = 0;
        agv_velocity_t actual_vel = vc.actual;
        int pwm_fl = vc.pwm[0];
        int pwm_fr = vc.pwm[1];
        int pwm_rl = vc.pwm[2];
        int pwm_rr = vc.pwm[3];

        // ── 10b. Hard cutoff PWM ──────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (g_obstacle_enabled &&
           ((s_front_blocked && intent_fwd)   ||
            (s_back_blocked  && intent_bwd)   ||
            (s_left_blocked  && intent_left)  ||
            (s_right_blocked && intent_right))) {
            pwm_fl = 0; pwm_fr = 0; pwm_rl = 0; pwm_rr = 0;
        }

        motor_set(MOTOR_FL, pwm_fl);
        motor_set(MOTOR_FR, pwm_fr);
        motor_set(MOTOR_RL, pwm_rl);
        motor_set(MOTOR_RR, pwm_rr);

        // ── 11. Gửi status về H7 ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        traj_receiver_send_status(actual_vel.vx, actual_vel.vy, actual_vel.wz);

        // ── 12. Push log ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        const int pwm_out[4] = { pwm_fl, pwm_fr, pwm_rl, pwm_rr };
        push_log(t_now, vel, &vc, pwm_out, &s_imu, true);
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// app_main
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
void app_main(void) {

#ifdef TEST_MODE
    run_tests();
    return;
#endif

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "AGV starting...");

    // LED + buzzer trước imu_init để báo được lỗi IMU (RST BNO085 là GPIO1, buzzer GPIO17: không dùng chung)
    led_buzzer_init();
    led_set_color(COLOR_YELLOW);
    buzzer_beep(2700, 100);

    // Init peripherals
    wifi_init();
    motor_init();
    encoder_init();
    ibus_init();
    int imu_rc = imu_init();   // chặn tối đa IMU_INIT_TIMEOUT_MS
    if (imu_rc != IMU_INIT_OK) {
        g_imu_fail = true;     // motor không bao giờ bật; led_task báo; log mang imu_init (§7, §8)
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "IMU_INIT_FAIL %d (%s): motors stay disabled", imu_rc, imu_init_err_str(imu_rc));
    }
    ultrasonic_init();
    traj_receiver_init();
    logger_init();

    // Init controller
    vel_ctrl_init();
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Controller: %s", CTRL_MODE == CTRL_PID ? "PID" : "ADRC");

    motor_enable(false);
    g_us_mutex = xSemaphoreCreateMutex();

    if (!g_imu_fail) {
        buzzer_beep(2700, 100);
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(100));
        buzzer_beep(2700, 100);
        led_set_color(COLOR_WHITE);
        ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Boot done — WAITING");
    } else {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Boot done — IMU FAIL STATE");
    }

    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(ibus_task,       "ibus",       4096, NULL, 4, NULL, 0);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(traj_task,       "traj",       4096, NULL, 3, NULL, 0);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(led_task,        "led",        4096, NULL, 2, NULL, 0);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(ultrasonic_task, "ultrasonic", 4096, NULL, 1, NULL, 0);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(logger_task,     "logger",     4096, NULL, 1, NULL, 0);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(encoder_task,    "encoder",    4096, NULL, 6, NULL, 1);
    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(control_task,    "control",    4096, NULL, 5, NULL, 1);

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "All tasks started");
}
===== FILE: AGV_MECANUM/src/logger/logger.h =====
#ifndef LOGGER_H
#define LOGGER_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Config — TODO: đổi IP laptop
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
#define LOGGER_PC_IP        "192.168.10.114"
#define LOGGER_UDP_PORT     5005
#define LOGGER_QUEUE_SIZE   32      // buffer 32 frame ở 100Hz = 320ms headroom

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Log data struct
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
typedef struct {
    int64_t timestamp_us;

    // Encoder
    int32_t fl_count, fr_count, rl_count, rr_count;
    float   fl_rpm,   fr_rpm,   rl_rpm,   rr_rpm;

    // Velocity actual (forward kinematics)
    float vx, vy, wz;

    // Setpoint (từ IBus hoặc RPi5)
    float sp_vx, sp_vy, sp_wz;

    // PWM command (-1000 ~ +1000)
    int pwm_fl, pwm_fr, pwm_rl, pwm_rr;

    // IMU — accelerometer (m/s²)
    float ax, ay, az;

    // IMU — gyroscope (rad/s)
    float gx, gy, gz;

    // IMU — euler (degrees)
    float yaw, pitch, roll;

    // Bộ đếm cộng dồn của vel_ctrl (spec S02): tick, Σ|Δpwm|, số tick bão hòa
    uint32_t tick;
    uint32_t dpwm_fl, dpwm_fr, dpwm_rl, dpwm_rr;
    uint32_t sat_fl,  sat_fr,  sat_rl,  sat_rr;

    // ── Spec controller–IMU v2.2 §8 (28/10). Cột cũ giữ tên: wz = wz encoder (FK),
    //    gz = gyro thô của chip, yaw = độ. ─────────────────────────────────────
    uint8_t  kind;             // LOG_KIND_DATA / LOG_KIND_RUN_END (dòng tổng kết, gửi 3 lần)
    // Mất log khép kín: điền trong logger_push / logger_task, người gọi không cần đặt
    uint32_t log_seq;          // tăng mỗi lần logger_push từ lúc khởi động, không reset
    uint32_t log_qdrop;        // số dòng mất vì hàng đợi đầy (tích lũy)
    uint32_t log_send_err;     // số lần sendto lỗi (tích lũy)
    uint8_t  motor_on;         // 0: dòng nhịp thưa 10 Hz khi motor tắt
    int8_t   imu_init;         // mã imu_init() (0 = đạt)
    // Hợp nhất IMU / vòng hướng (imu_fusion.c, heading.c)
    float    wz_meas, wz_cmd, gz_fused, yaw_rad;
    float    age_g_ms, age_r_ms;
    uint32_t gyro_n, rv_n, reset_n;
    uint8_t  g_valid, r_valid, wz_src, head_on, slip_flag;
    uint32_t src_switch_count, psi_reset_count, slip_on_count;
    float    psi_ref, e_psi, r, bias_gz;
} log_data_t;

#define LOG_KIND_DATA    0
#define LOG_KIND_RUN_END 1

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// API
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
void logger_init(void);
void logger_push(const log_data_t *data);   // non-blocking, gọi từ control_task; điền log_seq, log_qdrop
uint32_t logger_seq(void);                  // log_seq của dòng push gần nhất
uint32_t logger_qdrop(void);
uint32_t logger_send_err(void);
void logger_task(void *pv);                 // tạo task trong app_main, core 0

#endif // LOGGER_H

===== FILE: AGV_MECANUM/src/logger/logger.c =====
#include "logger.h"
#include "wifi/wifi.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "lwip/sockets.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"
#include "freertos/queue.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <fcntl.h>

static const char *TAG = "LOGGER";

static int              sock = -1;
static struct sockaddr_in dest_addr;
static QueueHandle_t    log_queue;

// Mất log khép kín (spec controller–IMU v2.2 §8): log_seq tăng mỗi lần push (kể cả khi hàng đợi
// đầy, nên PC thấy chỗ hổng), log_qdrop đếm dòng không vào được hàng đợi, log_send_err đếm sendto lỗi.
static uint32_t          s_log_seq;
static uint32_t          s_log_qdrop;
static volatile uint32_t s_log_send_err;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// logger_init — chỉ tạo queue, KHÔNG tạo socket
// Socket tạo trong logger_task sau khi WiFi connect
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
void logger_init(void) {
    log_queue = xQueueCreate(LOGGER_QUEUE_SIZE, sizeof(log_data_t));
    if (log_queue == NULL) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Queue create failed");
        return;
    }

    dest_addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    dest_addr.sin_port   = htons(LOGGER_UDP_PORT);
    inet_pton(AF_INET, LOGGER_PC_IP, &dest_addr.sin_addr);

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Logger init done → %s:%d", LOGGER_PC_IP, LOGGER_UDP_PORT);
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// logger_push — non-blocking, drop nếu queue đầy
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
void logger_push(const log_data_t *data) {
    if (log_queue == NULL || data == NULL) return;
    log_data_t d = *data;
    d.log_seq   = ++s_log_seq;
    d.log_qdrop = s_log_qdrop;
    if (xQueueSend(log_queue, &d, 0) != pdTRUE) s_log_qdrop++;
}

uint32_t logger_seq(void)      { return s_log_seq; }
uint32_t logger_qdrop(void)    { return s_log_qdrop; }
uint32_t logger_send_err(void) { return s_log_send_err; }

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Tạo UDP socket non-blocking
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
static bool create_socket(void) {
    sock = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, IPPROTO_UDP);
    if (sock < 0) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Socket create failed");
        return false;
    }
    int flags = fcntl(sock, F_GETFL, 0);
    fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, flags | O_NONBLOCK);
    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "UDP socket created");
    return true;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────
// logger_task — đợi WiFi, tạo socket, drain queue
// ─────────────────────────────────────────
void logger_task(void *pv) {
    // Đợi WiFi connect
    int wait_count = 0;
    while (!wifi_is_connected()) {
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(500));
        if (++wait_count % 10 == 0) {
            ESP_LOGW(TAG, "Waiting for WiFi...");
        }
    }

    if (!create_socket()) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Logger task exit — no socket");
        vTaskDelete(NULL);
        return;
    }

    // Gửi CSV header 1 lần để PC biết column order
    const char *header =
        "timestamp_us,"
        "fl_count,fr_count,rl_count,rr_count,"
        "fl_rpm,fr_rpm,rl_rpm,rr_rpm,"
        "vx,vy,wz,"
        "sp_vx,sp_vy,sp_wz,"
        "pwm_fl,pwm_fr,pwm_rl,pwm_rr,"
        "ax,ay,az,"
        "gx,gy,gz,"
        "yaw,pitch,roll,"
        "tick,dpwm_fl,dpwm_fr,dpwm_rl,dpwm_rr,sat_fl,sat_fr,sat_rl,sat_rr,"
        "log_seq,log_qdrop,log_send_err,motor_on,imu_init,"
        "wz_meas,wz_cmd,gz_fused,yaw_rad,age_g_ms,age_r_ms,gyro_n,rv_n,reset_n,"
        "g_valid,r_valid,wz_src,src_switch_count,psi_ref,e_psi,head_on,psi_reset_count,"
        "slip_flag,slip_on_count,r,bias_gz\n";
    sendto(sock, header, strlen(header), 0,
           (struct sockaddr *)&dest_addr, sizeof(dest_addr));

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "Logger streaming → %s:%d", LOGGER_PC_IP, LOGGER_UDP_PORT);

    log_data_t data;
    char buf[768];

    while (1) {
        // Block cho đến khi có data — không busy-wait
        if (xQueueReceive(log_queue, &data, portMAX_DELAY) != pdTRUE) continue;
        if (sock < 0) continue;

        if (data.kind == LOG_KIND_RUN_END) {
            // Dòng tổng kết cuối lượt (bắt đầu bằng '#': PC tách khỏi CSV)
            int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "#run_end,seq=%lu,last_data_seq=%lu,log_qdrop=%lu,log_send_err=%lu\n",
                             (unsigned long)data.log_seq, (unsigned long)data.tick,
                             (unsigned long)data.log_qdrop, (unsigned long)s_log_send_err);
            if (sendto(sock, buf, n, 0, (struct sockaddr *)&dest_addr, sizeof(dest_addr)) < 0)
                s_log_send_err++;
            continue;
        }

        int len = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf),
            "%lld,"
            "%ld,%ld,%ld,%ld,"
            "%.2f,%.2f,%.2f,%.2f,"
            "%.3f,%.3f,%.3f,"
            "%.3f,%.3f,%.3f,"
            "%d,%d,%d,%d,"
            "%.3f,%.3f,%.3f,"
            "%.3f,%.3f,%.3f,"
            "%.2f,%.2f,%.2f,"
            "%lu,%lu,%lu,%lu,%lu,%lu,%lu,%lu,%lu,"
            "%lu,%lu,%lu,%u,%d,"
            "%.4f,%.4f,%.4f,%.4f,%.1f,%.1f,%lu,%lu,%lu,"
            "%u,%u,%u,%lu,%.4f,%.4f,%u,%lu,"
            "%u,%lu,%.4f,%.5f\n",
            data.timestamp_us,
            data.fl_count, data.fr_count, data.rl_count, data.rr_count,
            data.fl_rpm,   data.fr_rpm,   data.rl_rpm,   data.rr_rpm,
            data.vx,  data.vy,  data.wz,
            data.sp_vx, data.sp_vy, data.sp_wz,
            data.pwm_fl, data.pwm_fr, data.pwm_rl, data.pwm_rr,
            data.ax, data.ay, data.az,
            data.gx, data.gy, data.gz,
            data.yaw, data.pitch, data.roll,
            (unsigned long)data.tick,
            (unsigned long)data.dpwm_fl, (unsigned long)data.dpwm_fr,
            (unsigned long)data.dpwm_rl, (unsigned long)data.dpwm_rr,
            (unsigned long)data.sat_fl,  (unsigned long)data.sat_fr,
            (unsigned long)data.sat_rl,  (unsigned long)data.sat_rr,
            (unsigned long)data.log_seq, (unsigned long)data.log_qdrop,
            (unsigned long)s_log_send_err, (unsigned)data.motor_on, (int)data.imu_init,
            data.wz_meas, data.wz_cmd, data.gz_fused, data.yaw_rad, data.age_g_ms, data.age_r_ms,
            (unsigned long)data.gyro_n, (unsigned long)data.rv_n, (unsigned long)data.reset_n,
            (unsigned)data.g_valid, (unsigned)data.r_valid, (unsigned)data.wz_src,
            (unsigned long)data.src_switch_count, data.psi_ref, data.e_psi,
            (unsigned)data.head_on, (unsigned long)data.psi_reset_count,
            (unsigned)data.slip_flag, (unsigned long)data.slip_on_count, data.r, data.bias_gz
        );

        if (len > 0 && len < (int)sizeof(buf)) {
            if (sendto(sock, buf, len, 0,
                       (struct sockaddr *)&dest_addr, sizeof(dest_addr)) < 0)
                s_log_send_err++;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: AGV_MECANUM/src/tests/test_imu_log.c =====
#include "test_imu_log.h"
#include "imu/imu.h"
#include "motor/motor.h"
#include "driver/uart.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/queue.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// IMU raw log (build with -DTEST_MODE -DTEST_IMU_LOG; envs imu_log_20, imu_log_100).
//
// UART0 at 921600 baud, ESP log output off. After boot the ESP32 prints
//   IMU READY v2 period_us=<IMU_REPORT_PERIOD_US>
// then ONE CSV line PER REPORT received from the BNO085 (no change filter, no polling):
//   kind,t_us,n,v0,v1,v2,qdrop
//   kind  A = accelerometer (m/s^2), G = calibrated gyro (rad/s), R = game RV euler (deg)
//   t_us  host receive time (esp_timer); the library gives no usable chip timestamp
//   n     running count of that report since boot (host side)
//   qdrop lines lost because the print queue was full (cumulative)
// imu.cpp hands each report to imu_report_hook in the library's callback task; the hook
// only copies it into a queue, this task formats and prints. Motors are disabled (EN = 0).
// v1 (21/10) printed only lines where a value changed, polled every 10 ms: that biased the
// noise statistics (repeated quantised values were dropped) and could miss 100 Hz reports.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#define IL_UART  UART_NUM_0
#define IL_BAUD  921600
#define IL_QLEN  256

typedef struct {
    uint8_t  kind;
    int64_t  t_us;
    uint32_t n;
    float    v[3];
} il_rec_t;

static QueueHandle_t     s_q;
static volatile uint32_t s_qdrop;

static void out(const char *s) { uart_write_bytes(IL_UART, s, strlen(s)); }

static void hook(uint8_t rpt_id, int64_t t_us, uint32_t n, const float v[4]) {
    il_rec_t r;
    r.kind = (rpt_id == IMU_RPT_ACCEL) ? 'A' : (rpt_id == IMU_RPT_GYRO) ? 'G' : 'R';
    r.t_us = t_us;
    r.n    = n;
    r.v[0] = v[0]; r.v[1] = v[1]; r.v[2] = v[2];
    if (xQueueSend(s_q, &r, 0) != pdTRUE) s_qdrop++;
}

void test_imu_log(void) {
    motor_init();                         /* EN = 0: drivers off during the test */
    esp_log_level_set("*", ESP_LOG_NONE);
    uart_driver_install(IL_UART, 256, 8192, 0, NULL, 0);
    uart_set_baudrate(IL_UART, IL_BAUD);

    s_q = xQueueCreate(IL_QLEN, sizeof(il_rec_t));
    configASSERT(s_q);
    imu_set_report_hook(hook);
    int rc = imu_init();

    // Chỉ in IMU READY khi khởi tạo đạt (spec controller–IMU v2.2 §7, C2-N1); lỗi thì in mã lỗi
    // rồi vẫn ghi mọi report tới được (để gỡ lỗi), công cụ PC không thấy IMU READY nên dừng.
    char line[160];
    if (rc == IMU_INIT_OK)
        snprintf(line, sizeof line, "IMU READY v2 period_us=%lu init_ms=%lu\n",
                 (unsigned long)IMU_REPORT_PERIOD_US, (unsigned long)imu_init_ms());
    else
        snprintf(line, sizeof line, "IMU INIT FAIL %d %s init_ms=%lu\n",
                 rc, imu_init_err_str(rc), (unsigned long)imu_init_ms());
    out(line);

    il_rec_t r;
    while (1) {
        if (xQueueReceive(s_q, &r, portMAX_DELAY) == pdTRUE) {
            const char *fmt = (r.kind == 'R') ? "%c,%lld,%lu,%.3f,%.3f,%.3f,%lu\n"
                                              : "%c,%lld,%lu,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%lu\n";
            int n = snprintf(line, sizeof line, fmt, r.kind, (long long)r.t_us,
                             (unsigned long)r.n, r.v[0], r.v[1], r.v[2], (unsigned long)s_qdrop);
            uart_write_bytes(IL_UART, line, n);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: AGV_MECANUM/src/tests/test_openloop.c =====
#include "test_openloop.h"
#include "encoder/encoder.h"
#include "motor/motor.h"
#include "imu/imu.h"
#include "driver/uart.h"
#include "driver/gpio.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"
#include "esp_log.h"
#include "esp_heap_caps.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Spec S01 open-loop test (wheels lifted). Build with -DTEST_MODE -DTEST_OPENLOOP.
//
// Link: UART0 (the USB-UART port used by `pio device monitor`), 921600 baud,
// text lines ending in '\n'. ESP log output is turned off so it does not mix
// with the data. EN (GPIO 45) is shared by all four drivers.
//
// PC -> ESP32
//   PING                    -> PONG
//   CLR                     clear the schedule                 -> OK CLR
//   SEG <pwm> <en> <dur_ms> append a segment (pwm -1000..1000, en 0/1)
//                                                              -> OK SEG <i>
//   RUN <wheel>             run the schedule on wheel 0..3 (FL, FR, RL, RR)
//                                                              -> OK RUN
//   H                       heartbeat; required at least every 500 ms during a run
//   STOP                    abort now
// ESP32 -> PC after a run
//   DONE n=<samples> aborted=<0|1> reason=<ok|stop|heartbeat|overflow> imu_n=<k> imu_overflow=<0|1>
//   <t_us>,<count>,<pwm>,<en>      one line per sample, t_us from run start
//   I,<t_us>,<kind>,<v0>,<v1>,<v2> one line per IMU report during the run (kind A accel m/s^2,
//                                  G gyro rad/s, R game RV euler deg; t_us = host receive time
//                                  from run start)  [v2, 28/10: every firmware has the IMU, QD 23]
//   END
//
// Boot: "OL READY v2 imu=<code> init_ms=<ms>". imu_init() failed (code != 0) -> every RUN
// answers "ERR imu <code>" (spec controller-IMU v2.2 section 7: stop and report).
//
// Safety: motors are off (EN=0, PWM 0) at boot, after every run and on abort.
// A run aborts if no line arrives for 500 ms, on STOP, or if the buffer fills.
// Brake vs coast: PWM 0 with EN=1 drives both half-bridges low (short-circuit
// brake on BTS7960-type drivers); EN=0 leaves the motor open (free coast).
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

#define OL_UART            UART_NUM_0
#define OL_BAUD            921600
#define OL_PERIOD_US       1000
#define OL_MAX_SAMPLES     12000            /* 12 s at 1 kHz */
#define OL_MAX_SEGS        8
#define OL_MAX_RUN_MS      12000
#define OL_HB_TIMEOUT_US   500000

typedef struct { int16_t pwm; uint8_t en; uint32_t dur_ms; } ol_seg_t;
typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) { uint32_t t_us; int32_t count; int16_t pwm; uint8_t en; } ol_sample_t;
typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) { uint32_t t_us; char kind; float v[3]; } ol_imu_t;

#define OL_MAX_IMU         1024             /* 12 s x 20 Hz x 3 reports = 720 */
static ol_imu_t    *s_ibuf = NULL;
static volatile int s_in;
static volatile bool s_ioverflow;
static int          s_imu_rc = -1;

static ol_seg_t     s_segs[OL_MAX_SEGS];
static uint32_t     s_seg_end_ms[OL_MAX_SEGS];
static int          s_nseg = 0;
static ol_sample_t *s_buf  = NULL;

static volatile int      s_wheel;
static volatile int      s_seg;
static volatile int      s_n;
static volatile bool     s_running, s_abort_req;
static volatile int      s_reason;          /* 0 ok, 1 stop, 2 heartbeat, 3 overflow */
static volatile int64_t  s_t0, s_last_hb;
static esp_timer_handle_t s_timer;

static const char *REASON[] = {"ok", "stop", "heartbeat", "overflow"};

static void out(const char *s) { uart_write_bytes(OL_UART, s, strlen(s)); }

static void motors_safe(void) {
    for (int i = 0; i < MOTOR_COUNT; i++) motor_set((motor_id_t)i, 0);
    gpio_set_level(GPIO_EN, 0);
}

static void finish(int reason) {
    motors_safe();
    s_reason  = reason;
    s_running = false;
}

// 1 kHz, esp_timer task context: apply the current segment, then sample.
static void ol_tick(void *arg) {
    if (!s_running) return;
    int64_t now = esp_timer_get_time();
    uint32_t t_ms = (uint32_t)((now - s_t0) / 1000);

    if (s_abort_req)                              { finish(1); return; }
    if (now - s_last_hb > OL_HB_TIMEOUT_US)      { finish(2); return; }
    while (s_seg < s_nseg && t_ms >= s_seg_end_ms[s_seg]) s_seg++;
    if (s_seg >= s_nseg)                          { finish(0); return; }
    if (s_n >= OL_MAX_SAMPLES)                    { finish(3); return; }

    const ol_seg_t *g = &s_segs[s_seg];
    gpio_set_level(GPIO_EN, g->en ? 1 : 0);
    motor_set((motor_id_t)s_wheel, g->en ? g->pwm : 0);

    ol_sample_t *p = &s_buf[s_n];
    p->t_us  = (uint32_t)(now - s_t0);
    p->count = encoder_get_count((enc_id_t)s_wheel);
    p->pwm   = g->en ? g->pwm : 0;
    p->en    = g->en;
    s_n++;
}

// IMU report hook (library callback task): keep the reports of the running schedule
static void ol_imu_hook(uint8_t rpt_id, int64_t t_us, uint32_t n, const float v[4]) {
    (void)n;
    if (!s_running) return;
    if (s_in >= OL_MAX_IMU) { s_ioverflow = true; return; }
    ol_imu_t *p = &s_ibuf[s_in];
    p->t_us = (uint32_t)(t_us - s_t0);
    p->kind = (rpt_id == IMU_RPT_ACCEL) ? 'A' : (rpt_id == IMU_RPT_GYRO) ? 'G' : 'R';
    p->v[0] = v[0]; p->v[1] = v[1]; p->v[2] = v[2];
    s_in++;
}

static void send_samples(void) {
    char line[96];
    snprintf(line, sizeof line, "DONE n=%d aborted=%d reason=%s imu_n=%d imu_overflow=%d\n",
             s_n, s_reason != 0, REASON[s_reason], s_in, s_ioverflow ? 1 : 0);
    out(line);
    for (int i = 0; i < s_n; i++) {
        const ol_sample_t *p = &s_buf[i];
        int len = snprintf(line, sizeof line, "%lu,%ld,%d,%u\n",
                           (unsigned long)p->t_us, (long)p->count, p->pwm, p->en);
        uart_write_bytes(OL_UART, line, len);
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < s_in; i++) {
        const ol_imu_t *p = &s_ibuf[i];
        int len = snprintf(line, sizeof line, "I,%lu,%c,%.5f,%.5f,%.5f\n",
                           (unsigned long)p->t_us, p->kind, p->v[0], p->v[1], p->v[2]);
        uart_write_bytes(OL_UART, line, len);
    }
    out("END\n");
}

static void start_run(int wheel) {
    uint32_t total = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < s_nseg; i++) { total += s_segs[i].dur_ms; s_seg_end_ms[i] = total; }
    if (wheel < 0 || wheel >= MOTOR_COUNT)  { out("ERR wheel\n"); return; }
    if (s_nseg == 0)                        { out("ERR no segments\n"); return; }
    if (total > OL_MAX_RUN_MS)              { out("ERR too long\n"); return; }
    if (s_imu_rc != IMU_INIT_OK) {
        char msg[32];
        snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "ERR imu %d\n", s_imu_rc);
        out(msg);
        return;
    }

    encoder_clear((enc_id_t)wheel);
    s_wheel = wheel; s_seg = 0; s_n = 0; s_reason = 0; s_abort_req = false;
    s_in = 0; s_ioverflow = false;
    s_t0 = s_last_hb = esp_timer_get_time();
    s_running = true;
    out("OK RUN\n");

    // Read heartbeats / STOP while the timer runs the schedule.
    char ln[32]; int k = 0; uint8_t c;
    while (s_running) {
        if (uart_read_bytes(OL_UART, &c, 1, pdMS_TO_TICKS(10)) == 1) {
            if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
                ln[k] = 0;
                if (k > 0) {
                    s_last_hb = esp_timer_get_time();
                    if (strncmp(ln, "STOP", 4) == 0) s_abort_req = true;
                }
                k = 0;
            } else if (k < (int)sizeof ln - 1) {
                ln[k++] = (char)c;
            }
        }
    }
    motors_safe();
    send_samples();
}

static void handle(char *ln) {
    char msg[48];
    if (strcmp(ln, "PING") == 0) { out("PONG\n"); }
    else if (strcmp(ln, "CLR") == 0) { s_nseg = 0; out("OK CLR\n"); }
    else if (strncmp(ln, "SEG ", 4) == 0) {
        int pwm, en, dur;
        if (sscanf(ln + 4, "%d %d %d", &pwm, &en, &dur) != 3 || s_nseg >= OL_MAX_SEGS ||
            pwm < -PWM_MAX || pwm > PWM_MAX || (en != 0 && en != 1) || dur <= 0) {
            out("ERR seg\n");
            return;
        }
        s_segs[s_nseg] = (ol_seg_t){ .pwm = (int16_t)pwm, .en = (uint8_t)en, .dur_ms = (uint32_t)dur };
        snprintf(msg, sizeof msg, "OK SEG %d\n", s_nseg++);
        out(msg);
    }
    else if (strncmp(ln, "RUN ", 4) == 0) { start_run(atoi(ln + 4)); }
    else if (strcmp(ln, "STOP") == 0 || strcmp(ln, "H") == 0) { motors_safe(); out("OK\n"); }
    else { out("ERR cmd\n"); }
}

void test_openloop(void) {
    motor_init();                 /* leaves EN = 0 */
    encoder_init();
    motors_safe();

    s_buf  = heap_caps_malloc(OL_MAX_SAMPLES * sizeof(ol_sample_t), MALLOC_CAP_8BIT);
    s_ibuf = heap_caps_malloc(OL_MAX_IMU * sizeof(ol_imu_t), MALLOC_CAP_8BIT);

    esp_log_level_set("*", ESP_LOG_NONE);   /* keep UART0 for data only */
    imu_set_report_hook(ol_imu_hook);
    s_imu_rc = imu_init();                  /* at most IMU_INIT_TIMEOUT_MS */
    uart_driver_install(OL_UART, 1024, 4096, 0, NULL, 0);
    uart_set_baudrate(OL_UART, OL_BAUD);

    const esp_timer_create_args_t targs = { .callback = ol_tick, .name = "ol_tick" };
    esp_timer_create(&targs, &s_timer);
    esp_timer_start_periodic(s_timer, OL_PERIOD_US);

    if (s_buf == NULL || s_ibuf == NULL) {
        while (1) { out("ERR no memory\n"); vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(1000)); }
    }
    char ready[64];
    snprintf(ready, sizeof ready, "OL READY v2 imu=%d init_ms=%lu\n", s_imu_rc, (unsigned long)imu_init_ms());
    out(ready);

    char ln[64]; int k = 0; uint8_t c;
    while (1) {
        if (uart_read_bytes(OL_UART, &c, 1, pdMS_TO_TICKS(100)) != 1) continue;
        if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
            ln[k] = 0;
            if (k > 0) handle(ln);
            k = 0;
        } else if (k < (int)sizeof ln - 1) {
            ln[k++] = (char)c;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: AGV_MECANUM/components/esp32_BNO08x/source/BNO08xSH2HAL.cpp =====
/**
 * @file BNO08xSH2HAL.cpp
 * @author Myles Parfeniuk
 */

#include "BNO08xSH2HAL.hpp"
#include "BNO08x.hpp"

BNO08x* BNO08xSH2HAL::imu;

// AGV_MECANUM (28/10/2026, spec controller-IMU v2.2 §3): báo ứng dụng mỗi lần chip SH2_RESET.
// Bản thật ở src/imu/imu.cpp (tăng reset_n); bản weak này chỉ để thư viện link được một mình.
extern "C" __attribute__((weak)) void imu_note_chip_reset(void) {}

/**
 * @brief Sets the BNO08x driver object to be used with sh2 HAL lib callbacks.
 *
 * @param hal_imu Pointer to BNO08x driver object to be used with sh2 HAL lib callbacks.
 *
 * @return void, nothing to return
 */
void BNO08xSH2HAL::set_hal_imu(BNO08x* hal_imu)
{
    imu = hal_imu;
}

/**
 * @brief Opens SPI instance by waiting for interrupt.
 *
 * @return Always returns 0.
 */
int BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_open(sh2_Hal_t* self)
{
    spi_wait_for_int();

    return 0;
}

/**
 * @brief Closes SPI instance (nothing to do here, but required by sh2 HAL lib for cases where other
 * communication protocols are used.)
 *
 * @return void, nothing to return
 */
void BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_close(sh2_Hal_t* self)
{
    // do nothing
}

/**
 * @brief SPI rx callback for sh2 HAL lib.
 *
 * @param self sh2 HAL lib object being used with BNO08x driver instance.
 * @param pBuffer Buffer to store received packet.
 * @param len Length of bytes to read.
 * @param t_us Time in microseconds (unused, forced sh2 HAL lib required function type)
 *
 * @return Size of received packet in bytes, 0 on failure.
 */
int BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_read(sh2_Hal_t* self, uint8_t* pBuffer, unsigned len, uint32_t* t_us)
{
    uint16_t packet_sz = 0;

    // hint never asserted, fail transaction
    if (!spi_wait_for_int())
        return 0;

    // assert chip select
    gpio_set_level(imu->imu_config.io_cs, 0);

    packet_sz = spi_read_sh2_packet_header(pBuffer);

    if ((packet_sz > len) || (packet_sz == 0))
    {
        gpio_set_level(imu->imu_config.io_cs, 1);
        return 0;
    }

    packet_sz = spi_read_sh2_packet_body(pBuffer, packet_sz);

    // de-assert chip select
    gpio_set_level(imu->imu_config.io_cs, 1);

    return packet_sz;
}

/**
 * @brief SPI tx callback for sh2 HAL lib.
 *
 * @param self sh2 HAL lib object being used with BNO08x driver instance.
 * @param pBuffer Buffer containing data to write.
 * @param len Length in bytes to write.
 *
 * @return Size of sent data (len param), 0 on failure.
 */
int BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_write(sh2_Hal_t* self, uint8_t* pBuffer, unsigned len)
{
    // hint never asserted, fail transaction
    if (!spi_wait_for_int())
        return 0;

    // setup transaction to send packet
    imu->spi_transaction.length = len * 8;
    imu->spi_transaction.rxlength = 0;
    imu->spi_transaction.tx_buffer = pBuffer;
    imu->spi_transaction.rx_buffer = NULL;
    imu->spi_transaction.flags = 0;

    gpio_set_level(imu->imu_config.io_cs, 0);                         // assert chip select

    // send data packet
    if(spi_device_polling_transmit(imu->spi_hdl, &imu->spi_transaction) != ESP_OK)
        return 0;

    gpio_set_level(imu->imu_config.io_cs, 1);                         // de-assert chip select

    return len;
}

/**
 * @brief Get time in microseconds callback for sh2 HAL lib.
 *
 * @param self sh2 HAL lib object being used with BNO08x driver instance.
 *
 * @return Time in microseconds.
 */
uint32_t BNO08xSH2HAL::get_time_us(sh2_Hal_t* self)
{
    uint64_t time_us = esp_timer_get_time();

    if (time_us > UINT32_MAX)
        time_us -= UINT32_MAX;

    return static_cast<uint32_t>(time_us & 0xFFFFFFFFU);
}

/**
 * @brief General event callback for sh2 HAL lib, used to notify tasks of reset.
 *
 * @param cookie User set input parameter as void pointer (unused), see sh2_Open().
 * @param pEvent Pointer to asynchronous event.
 *
 * @return void, nothing to return
 */
void BNO08xSH2HAL::hal_cb(void* cookie, sh2_AsyncEvent_t* pEvent)
{
    if (pEvent->eventId == SH2_RESET)
    {
        imu_note_chip_reset();   // AGV_MECANUM: reset_n cho controller (spec controller-IMU v2.2 §3)
        xEventGroupSetBits(imu->sync_ctx.evt_grp_task, BNO08xPrivateTypes::EVT_GRP_BNO08x_TASK_RESET_OCCURRED);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Sensor event callback for sh2 HAL lib, sends received reports to data_proc_task().
 *
 * @param cookie User set input parameter as void pointer (unused), see sh2_Open().
 * @param event Pointer to sensor event.
 *
 * @return void, nothing to return
 */
void BNO08xSH2HAL::sensor_event_cb(void* cookie, sh2_SensorEvent_t* event)
{
    xQueueSend(imu->queue_rx_sensor_event, event, 0);
}

/**
 * @brief Hardware reset callback for sh2 HAL lib, toggle RST gpio.
 *
 * @return void, nothing to return
 */
void BNO08xSH2HAL::hardware_reset()
{
    imu->toggle_reset();
}

/**
 * @brief SPI wait for HINT sh2 HAL lib callback.
 *
 * @return True if interrupt was detected before timeout.
 */
bool BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_wait_for_int()
{
    if (imu->wait_for_hint() != ESP_OK)
    {
        hardware_reset();
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

/**
 * @brief SPI rx packet header (invoked from SPI rx callback.)
 *
 * @param pBuffer Buffer to store received header.
 *
 * @return Packet size (should always be 4), 0 on failure.
 */
uint16_t BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_read_sh2_packet_header(uint8_t* pBuffer)
{
    uint8_t dummy_header_tx[4] = {0};
    uint16_t packet_sz = 0;

    // setup transaction to receive first 4 bytes (packet header)
    imu->spi_transaction.rx_buffer = pBuffer;
    imu->spi_transaction.tx_buffer = dummy_header_tx;
    imu->spi_transaction.length = 4 * 8;
    imu->spi_transaction.rxlength = 4 * 8;
    imu->spi_transaction.flags = 0;

    if (spi_device_polling_transmit(imu->spi_hdl, &imu->spi_transaction) != ESP_OK)
        return 0;

    packet_sz = PARSE_PACKET_LENGTH(pBuffer);

    // clear continuation/batch bit
    packet_sz &= ~0x8000U;

    return packet_sz;
}

/**
 * @brief SPI rx packet body (invoked from SPI rx callback.)
 *
 * @param pBuffer Buffer to store received packet body.
 *
 * @return Packet size, 0 on failure.
 */
int BNO08xSH2HAL::spi_read_sh2_packet_body(uint8_t* pBuffer, uint16_t packet_sz)
{
    imu->spi_transaction.rx_buffer = pBuffer + 4;
    imu->spi_transaction.tx_buffer = NULL;
    imu->spi_transaction.length = packet_sz * 8;
    imu->spi_transaction.rxlength = packet_sz * 8;
    imu->spi_transaction.flags = 0;

    if (spi_device_polling_transmit(imu->spi_hdl, &imu->spi_transaction) != ESP_OK)
        return 0;
    else
        return packet_sz;
}
